Non-IFRS Accounting Standards adjusted net profit                                    非國際財務報告會計準則經調整純利
The non-IFRS Accounting Standards adjusted net profit has not been                   非國際財務報告會計準則經調整純利未按照國
calculated in accordance with the IFRS Accounting Standards, thus it is              際財務報告會計準則計算，視為非國際財務報
deemed as a non-IFRS Accounting Standards financial indicator. The non-              告會計準則的財務指標，其定義為除以股份為
IFRS Accounting Standards adjusted net profit refers to the net profit after         基礎的薪酬後純利，而非國際財務報告會計準
excluding share-based payment expenses, while the non-IFRS Accounting                則經調整純利率的定義為非國際財務報告會計
Standards adjusted net profit margin refers to the non-IFRS Accounting               準則經調整純利除以收益。我們認為，這些信
Standards adjusted net profit divided by revenue. We are of the view that            息對於投資者在不影響本集團經營業績或現金
such information is useful for investors to compare the results of the Group,        流的情況下比較本集團的業績是有用的，並允
provided that the results of operation or cash flows of the Group are not            許投資者考慮管理層在評估本集團業績時使用
being affected, and enables investors to take into consideration of the              的指標。投資者不應認為非國際財務報告會計
indicators used by the management when assessing the results of the Group.           準則的財務指標替代或優於本集團根據國際財
Investors shall not treat non-IFRS Accounting Standards financial indicator          務報告會計準則編製的業績。此外，可能不是
as an alternative or better version of the results of the Group prepared in          所有公司會以相同的方式計算該項非國際財務
accordance with IFRS Accounting Standards. In addition, not all companies            報告會計準則的財務指標，因此未必可與其他
will adopt the same way in calculating such non-IFRS Accounting Standards            公司採用的相若計量比較。
financial indicators. Hence, similar measurements made by other companies
may not be comparable.

     Share-based payment expenses are non-cash items, which do not directly           以股份為基礎的付款開支屬於非現金項目且並
     reflect our business operation. Hence, through eliminating the effects of such   無直接反應我們的業務營運。因此，通過撇除
     items on calculation of non-IFRS Accounting Standards adjusted net profit,       該等項目對計算非國際財務報告會計準則經調
     relevant operating performance can be better reflected, and it would be          整純利影響的舉措，可更好地反應我們的相關
     more convenient to compare operating performance in different periods.           經營表現，並更加便於比較不同期間的經營表

     As the Company’s subsidiaries operate in the PRC, Asia Pacific, Americas            本公司的附屬公司在中國、亞太、美洲和歐洲

     arising from certain currency exposure (mainly related to US dollar, Thai           關美元、泰銖、新加坡幣及港幣）引致的外匯風
     Baht, Singapore dollar and Hong Kong dollar). Our management considers              險。由於本集團以我們經營實體各自功能貨幣
     that the business is not exposed to any significant foreign exchange risk as        以外貨幣計值的金融資產及負債不重大，故管
     the financial assets and liabilities of our Group denominated in currencies         理層認為業務並無任何重大外匯風險敞口。本
     other than the respective functional currencies of our operating entities are       集團並沒有對沖外幣波動，但是會緊密地監控
     insignificant. Although the Group does not hedge against foreign currency           有關情況，並在必要時採取措施以保證外匯風

   (v) grants of Awards with a mixed vesting schedule such that the              (v) 授出的獎勵附帶混合歸屬時間表，令

  as consolidated and revised) of the Cayman Islands. The address of           曼群島註冊成立為獲豁免有限公司。本公
  the Company’s registered office is PO Box 309, Ugland House, Grand           司註冊辦事處的地址為PO Box 309, Ugland
  Cayman, KY1-1104, Cayman Islands.                                            House, Grand Cayman, KY1-1104, Cayman
                                                                               Islands。

  its subsidiaries (together, the “Group”) are principally engaged in the      公司（統稱「本集團」）主要於中華人民共和
  product design and development and sale of pop toys in the People’s          國，包括中華人民共和國香港、澳門及台

  Taiwan of the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”) and certain overseas    事潮流玩具的產品設計與開發及銷售。本
  countries and regions. The ultimate holding company of the Company is        公司的最終控股公司為GWF Holding Limited
  GWF Holding Limited (formerly known as Grant Wang Holding Limited),          （前稱為Grant Wang Holding Limited），後者

        4.1 Financial risk factors                                                      4.1 財務風險因素

            The Group’s activities expose it to a variety of financial risks: market      本集團的活動使其面臨各種財務風
            risk (including foreign exchange risk and cash flow and fair value            險：市場風險（包括外匯風險、現金流
            interest rate risk), credit risk and liquidity risk.                          量及公平值利率風險）、信貸風險及流
                                                                                          動資金風險。


            The interim condensed consolidated financial information does not             中期簡明綜合財務資料並不包括年度
            include all financial risk management information and disclosures             財務報表要求的所有財務風險管理資
            required in the annual financial statements, and should be read in            料及披露，應與本集團截至 2024 年 12
            conjunction with the Group’s annual financial statements for the              月31日止年度全年財務報表一併閱讀。
            year ended 31 December 2024.


            There have been no material changes in the risk management                    自2024 年12 月31 日起，風險管理政策
            policies since 31 December 2024.                                              並無重大變動。


        4.2 Credit risk                                                                 4.2 信貸風險

            Credit risk is the risk of suffering financial loss, should any of the        信貸風險指若本集團的顧客 ╱ 客戶或
            Group’s customers/clients or counterparties fail to fulfil their              交易對手未能履行其對本集團的合約
            contractual obligations to the Group. Credit risk of the Group arises         責任而蒙受財務損失的風險。本集團
            mainly from exposure of trade receivables and other receivables               的信用風險主要來自於從事經營活動
            raised by the subsidiaries engaged in operating activities.                   的附屬公司之貿易應收款項及其他應
                                                                                          收款項。


            The Group adopts the “expected credit loss” model on its                      本集團按照國際財務報告準則第9 號
            impairment assessment of debt instruments which are measured                 「金融工具」的條文，對以攤銷成本計
            at amortised cost, in accordance with the provisions of IFRS 9                量的債務工具減值評估採用「預期信貸
            “Financial Instrument”.                                                       虧損」模型。

        regions other than the Mainland China, Hong Kong, Macau and Taiwan)         關的評價已向主要經營決策者逐一匯報。

     21 SHARE-BASED COMPENSATION                                                    21 以股份為基礎的報酬
        The Company adopted the Share Award Scheme on 24 July 2020                   本公司於 2020 年 7 月 24 日採納股份獎勵計
        with a duration of 10 years commencing from the adoption date. The           劃，自採納日期起計為期 10 年。股份獎勵
        objective of the Share Award Scheme is to align the interests of selected    計劃之目的乃使選定參與者的利益與本集
        participants with those of the Group and to encourage and retain             團利益保持一致，鼓勵及挽留選定參與者
        selected participants to make contributions to the long-term growth          為本集團長遠增長及溢利作出貢獻。
        and profits of the Group.


        Under the Share Award Scheme, Pop Mart Partner Limited (the “Share           根據股份獎勵計劃，Pop Mart Partner
        Award Trust”) was established in British Virgin Islands on 7 May 2020.       Limited（「股份獎勵信託」）於2020年5月7日
        As the financial and operational policies of the Share Award Trust are       在英屬維京群島成立。由於股份獎勵信託
        governed by the Group, and the Group benefits from the Share Award           的財務及運營政策受本集團管理，且本集
        Trust’s activities, the Share Award Trust is consolidated in the Group’s     團從股份獎勵信託的活動中受益，故股份
        financial statements as a special purpose entity.                            獎勵信託於本集團財務報表綜合入賬為特
                                                                                     殊目的實體。


        The aggregate number of RSU will not exceed 10% shares without               在未經股東批准下，受限制股份單位數目
        shareholders’ approval subject to an annual limit of 5% of the total         合共不得超過10%的股份，並受於採納日期
        number of issued shares as at the date of adoption.                          已發行股份總數5%的年度限額規限。


        On 24 July 2020, the board of directors of the Company approved to           於2020年7月24日，本公司董事會批准以零
        issue 2,442,873 ordinary shares of the Company to the Share Award            代價向股份獎勵信託發行2,442,873股本公司
        Trust for nil consideration in relation to Share Award Scheme. The           普通股，用於股份獎勵計劃的相關事宜。
        above issuance of shares was completed on 28 July 2020, and the RSU          上述股份發行已於2020年7月28日完成，股
        held by the Share Award Trust was recorded as “Shares held for Share         份獎勵信託持有的受限制股份單位將列賬
        Award Scheme” and deducted from equity until the RSU are vested              為「就股份獎勵計劃持有的股份」，並從權
        or cancelled. The number of shares under the Share Award Scheme              益中扣除，直至受限制股份單位歸屬或註
        have been changed to 24,428,730 after the capitalisation issue on 11         銷。於2020 年12 月11 日資本化發行後，股
        December 2020.                                                               份獎勵計劃下的股份數量已變為24,428,730
                                                                                     股。


        Pursuant to the Share Award Scheme, the Group has granted RSUs to            根據股份獎勵計劃，本集團向選定參與者
        selected participants which include employees of the Group, certain          （包括本集團僱員、若干第三方供應商及一

29 RELATED PARTY TRANSACTIONS                                                       29 關聯方交易
   Parties are considered to be related if one party has the ability, directly         若一方有能力直接或間接控制另一方，或
   or indirectly, control the other party or exercise significant influence            在作出財務及經營決策時對另一方發揮重
   over the other party in making financial and operation decisions. Parties           大影響，即視為有關聯。倘所涉各方受共
   are also considered to be related if they are subject to common control.            同控制，則亦視為有關聯。本集團的主要
   Members of key management of the Group and their close family                       管理層成員及其近親成員亦被視為關聯方。
   members are also considered as related parties.


   Save as disclosed elsewhere in this interim condensed consolidated                  除本中期簡明綜合財務資料其他章節所披
   financial information, the Group has the following significant related              露者外，本集團亦有以下重大關聯交易，
   transactions which were carried out in the ordinary course of the Group’s           該等交易乃於本集團日常業務過程中進
   business and were determined based on mutually agreed terms.                        行，並按共同商定的條款釐定。


   All the transaction with related parties were conducted in accordance               與關聯方的所有交易均按照本集團與關聯
   with the terms of contracts entered into between the Group and the                  方訂立的合約條款進行。
   related parties.
